IGUI_KBW_GuideAdminRulesBody::en = " <H2> Safe draft workflow <TEXT> <LINE> <LINE> Authorized administrators open Build Rules from the ElyonLib dock. The editor copies the synchronized server document into a private draft; browsing, loading a preset, and changing controls do not affect players. Validate and Save must succeed before the server publishes a new revision. <LINE> <LINE> <H2> Editor layout <TEXT> <LINE> <LINE> Select a category, subcategory, or buildable on the left, then select a stage and tab on the right. Availability, inputs, finishes, skills and XP, recipe knowledge, and time are separate controls. The Finishes tab can waive plaster, paint, or wallpaper materials for every wall, while selected walls can explicitly inherit, require, or waive each cost. Non-wall paintable objects keep their normal requirements. Reload discards the draft and requests current server data. Reset Selected removes that buildable's overrides. <LINE> <LINE> Concurrent saves use revision checks. If another admin saves first, reload the newer revision before applying your changes.",
